# Agents · C · part 45

962 entries, correctness-reviewer to council-seat. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **correctness-reviewer**: Read-only correctness sub-reviewer for the 4man Reviewer. Audits a diff for logical correctness — boundaries, null/empty handling, error paths, races, state, idempotency, data integrity, and the spec's edge cases — and reports issues with a concrete failing scenario. ([lzlrd/marketplace](https://github.com/lzlrd/marketplace/blob/HEAD/4man/agents/correctness-reviewer.md))
- **correctness-reviewer**: Independent read-only reviewer of commits for correctness - each change against the original executable's evidence, shared code paths, other games of the same engine, edge cases. Use in the double review, without the author's conclusions. ([Marketto/retro-engine-forge](https://github.com/Marketto/retro-engine-forge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/correctness-reviewer.md))
- **correctness-reviewer**: Relit un diff sous le seul angle de la correction - répond-il à la demande, traite-t-il la cause racine, tient-il sur les cas limites et les régressions. Lecture seule. Lancé en parallèle par le skill review sur un gros diff, jamais directement. ([maximejolivet/max-coding-agents-skills](https://github.com/maximejolivet/max-coding-agents-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/correctness-reviewer.md))

- **corretora-seguros-saas**: Use este subagente quando precisar mapear coberturas de seguro RC profissional + cyber pra SaaS regulado BR, montar planilha de cotação, comparar propostas de seguradoras, ou preparar pedido pra corretora SUSEP humana. NÃO emite apólice — corretora licenciada SUSEP é obrigatória por lei. ([roldaobatista/Certificado-Calibracao](https://github.com/roldaobatista/Certificado-Calibracao/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/corretora-seguros-saas.md))
- **corridor-mcp**: **Corridor is not used for HoloScript / HoloMesh development in this ecosystem.** ([brianonbased-dev/HoloScript](https://github.com/brianonbased-dev/HoloScript/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/corridor-mcp.md) · ★12)
- **corridor-researcher**: Use this subagent when you need to research a new corridor before building its page for paidacross. Invoke it with a corridor, for example "research USD to PHP" or "research the Nigeria corridor". It gathers providers, receiving methods, fees, exchange rate behavior, compliance documents, regulators, limits, timelines, and the real questions people ask, all from primary sources, then returns a structured research brief for the main session to build the page from. It is read-only, it returns data and does not create or edit files. ([resmi360io/nomad-stack](https://github.com/resmi360io/nomad-stack/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/corridor-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **corridor-reviewer**: Use this subagent to fact-check a corridor-verifier change list before it is applied to a live paidacross page. Invoke it with the verifier's JSON change list and the corridor it covers. It independently re-researches every proposed change against primary sources and returns an approve, amend, or reject verdict per item, with corrected text where the verifier got something wrong. It is the quality gate between research and publication. Read-only: it judges and corrects, it never edits files. ([resmi360io/nomad-stack](https://github.com/resmi360io/nomad-stack/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/corridor-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **corridor-verifier**: Use this subagent to research and verify the factual correctness of a paidacross corridor page against primary sources. Invoke it with a corridor path or currency pair, for example "verify /receive/usd-to-pkr" or "check the PKR corridor". It reads the page in the repo, checks every fee, rate, provider, compliance, and regulator claim against official sources, and returns a change list of anything outdated or wrong, with sources. It is read-only: it reports findings and never edits files. Applying reviewer-approved changes is the orchestrating session's job. ([resmi360io/nomad-stack](https://github.com/resmi360io/nomad-stack/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/corridor-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **corridra-architect**: Engineering design stage of a Corridra squad run. Turns a task packet into a build plan - module boundaries, services to reuse, exact files, blast radius for auth, RLS, money, events and migrations, the ADR when a contract changes, and a test plan mapped to every acceptance check. Use after corridra-product and before corridra-builder. Writes ADR files only, never application code or UI decisions. ([sadat99/corridra](https://github.com/sadat99/corridra/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/corridra-architect.md))
- **corridra-authority**: First stage of every Corridra squad run. Resolves which instructions, plans, ADRs and requirements govern a task today, from the repository at HEAD, and returns a stamped Authority Brief that every later stage must carry. Use before any planning, design, build, review or record work, and again whenever a brief's stamp no longer matches. Read only; never judges code or product quality. ([sadat99/corridra](https://github.com/sadat99/corridra/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/corridra-authority.md))
- **corridra-builder**: The only writer of Corridra application code, tests and migrations in a squad run. Implements an approved build plan and UI spec under a matching Authority Brief, runs focused checks on what it touched and commits on the designated branch. Use after corridra-architect (and corridra-designer for visible work), and to fix confirmed findings. Never edits the ledger, work log, plan register or ADRs. ([sadat99/corridra](https://github.com/sadat99/corridra/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/corridra-builder.md))
- **corridra-designer**: Design and experience owner for a Corridra squad run. Before a build, turns a task packet into a UI spec - layout, components, every state, copy, accessibility and widths - under the current UI direction and design system. After a build, audits the rendered result against that spec. Use for any visible change. Never writes code and never judges correctness, security or architecture. ([sadat99/corridra](https://github.com/sadat99/corridra/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/corridra-designer.md))
- **corridra-product**: Product stage of a Corridra squad run. Turns a request plus a stamped Authority Brief into a task packet - the outcome, affected requirements, everything that must be preserved, acceptance checks and the decisions only the owner can take. Use after corridra-authority and before architecture or design. Decides what and why, never how, never the UI. Read only. ([sadat99/corridra](https://github.com/sadat99/corridra/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/corridra-product.md))
- **corridra-reviewer**: Adversarial correctness reviewer for a Corridra squad run. Reads the diff against the Authority Brief, task packet and build plan and hunts for defects - wrong authority, broken preservation, tenant or RLS leaks, money or history damage, fabricated controls, weakened tests. Use after corridra-builder, in parallel with corridra-verifier, and to refute another reviewer's findings. Never fixes code, never runs release gates, never judges visual design. ([sadat99/corridra](https://github.com/sadat99/corridra/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/corridra-reviewer.md))
- **corridra-scribe**: Keeper of Corridra's shared record in a squad run. Writes the work-log entry, updates the ledger within its size cap, and keeps the plan status register and every plan's Document status line true, so the next run's authority resolution is correct. Use at the end of every run, and whenever corridra-authority reports register drift. Never edits application code, tests or ADRs. ([sadat99/corridra](https://github.com/sadat99/corridra/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/corridra-scribe.md))
- **corridra-verifier**: Proof stage of a Corridra squad run. Runs the repository's own checks on the frozen revision - preflight, every mandatory release gate, CI on the exact head and the deployment of a merged commit - and returns exact receipts. Use after corridra-builder, after every fix, and after a merge. Never edits source or tests and never judges design or code quality. ([sadat99/corridra](https://github.com/sadat99/corridra/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/corridra-verifier.md))
- **corroboration-analyst**: Use proactively after all artifact agents complete and before generate_report. Takes all findings from state.json and stress-tests each one against other artifact sources to confirm, escalate, demote, or dismiss. Applies evidence corroboration chains, stacked anomaly validation, and temporal proximity analysis. Returns a reviewed finding list with confidence adjustments and a defensible conclusion.
